For each test: the question, the request that was sent, what came back, and why that result is the right one.

How to read the cards

An MCP client only ever sees a generic tool error for a refused call, so an answer shown as ERROR is expected for refusals: the typed reason (for example SEMANTIC_PROFILE_UNVALIDATED or AccessDenied) is read from the audit row printed under it. Company identifiers appear as short hashes. Exact monetary figures are masked as <amount> in this public report; equality with the oracle is stated by the outcome, and the exact figures are kept in a private evidence file outside Git.

Meaning

PASSpassed
FINDINGa real difference or defect found
INCONCLUSIVEnot enough evidence to decide
EVIDENCE_REQUIREDexternal or native evidence is owed
CAPABILITY_UNSUPPORTEDthe product does not have this capability
SEMANTIC_PROFILE_UNVALIDATEDnumbers are correctly withheld until the profile is validated
REFUSED-WRITEa write was correctly refused
ERRORthe test could not run to a typed answer

Catalogue stories (ST) (90)

ST-001EVIDENCE_REQUIREDMonth-closed verdict plus blockersCA · P0 · EV

Question (business scenario)

Month-closed verdict plus blockers

Expected by the catalogue

NOT_CONFIRMED with missing definition/evidence, never "closed" from document presence

Negative semantics

closing doc exists but residual 811/821/216.1 remains

Request that was sent

Principaluser_company_oneToolexternal_evidence_manifest
Arguments
{"company_id": "guid:4bec391a", "evidence_id": "00000000000000000000000000000000", "source_id": "onec-818ha-reference"}
AnswerOK
{"business_acceptance": "NOT_EVALUATED", "keys": ["business_acceptance", "native_approval_inferred", "reason", "status"], "native_approval_inferred": false, "status": "CAPABILITY_UNSUPPORTED"}
Audit rowdenied/CAPABILITY_UNSUPPORTED; items=None

What was received

external_evidence_manifest(unknown evidence id, real company)CAPABILITY_UNSUPPORTEDtyped refusal; audit {'outcome': 'denied', 'detail_code': 'CAPABILITY_UNSUPPORTED', 'returned_items': None}; rows returned: False; business_acceptance=NOT_EVALUATED, native_approval_inferred=False
related NR-01INCONCLUSIVENative reconciliation: Month-close overall status

Why this result is correct

EVIDENCE_REQUIRED (external or native evidence is owed). The answer depends on external evidence (a hashed document, bank statement or declaration). The evidence plane refused with a typed status instead of inventing a value, so EVIDENCE_REQUIRED is the correct result.

Evidence classes: GATEWAY_OBSERVATION | Oracle: evidence plane

Owner action

Owner/accountant: supply the external evidence for this case (hashed PDF, bank statement or declaration) through the evidence plane; the lane never invents it.

Requirement gaps: REQ-GAP-01

ST-002CAPABILITY_UNSUPPORTEDActionable cleanup proposals, not a reportCA · P0 · UG

Question (business scenario)

Actionable cleanup proposals, not a report

Expected by the catalogue

each proposal cites rows, accounting+fiscal angle, human_review_required, no claim of execution

Negative semantics

proposal must not claim posted

Request that was sent

No request is sent: the capability is searched in the product surface (public MCP tool names and descriptions, admin API routes, CLI sub-commands) with the pattern shown in the observation.

What was received

product surface searchABSENTpattern /proposal|clean.?up|month.?close|rule.?pack/ over 23 public MCP tools, admin API routes and CLI commands: no match

Why this result is correct

CAPABILITY_UNSUPPORTED (the product does not have this capability). The capability was searched for in the whole product surface (public MCP tools and descriptions, admin API routes, CLI commands) and is not there. Reporting CAPABILITY_UNSUPPORTED is the truthful statement; inventing a pass would hide the gap.

Evidence classes: REPO_STATIC | Oracle: capability search

Owner action

Operator: scope-rebaseline decision for the missing capability (see the REQ-GAP references).

Requirement gaps: REQ-GAP-02
